# Drift — Task Tracker

> **Purpose:** Durable, resumable task list. This is the source of truth across sessions.
> **Created:** 2026-05-17
> **Companion to:** `master_roadmap.md`, `final_assessment.md`, `09-roadmap/execution_roadmap.md`
> **Operating contract:** All 15 rules from the project framing message apply to every task below.

---

## How to use this file

1. **Status is tracked in the checkbox** at the start of each line — never delete a task, only update its state.
2. **Stable IDs** (e.g. `QW-01`, `1.1`, `2.13`) are how we reference tasks across messages. Numeric IDs match `09-roadmap/execution_roadmap.md` for cross-reference.
3. **Sub-area headings** (`### 1.A`, `### 1.B`, …) are organisational only — they are NOT tracked tasks. Only checkbox lines are tracked.
4. **When resuming a session**, read this file first to know what's done and what's next.
5. **When a task completes**, update the checkbox here AND add a one-line completion note (commit hash / PR / file paths touched) under it.
6. **When a task is split or merged**, note it inline; never silently restructure the list.

### Status legend

| Mark | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `- [ ]` | Pending — not started |
| `- [/]` | In progress — currently being worked on |
| `- [x]` | Completed |
| `- [-]` | Skipped / superseded — keep the line, add a note explaining why |
| `- [!]` | Blocked — waiting on a decision or external input; add a note |

### Effort tags

XS = ≤ 1 hr · S = 1–4 hr · M = ½–1 day · L = 1–2 days · XL = 2–5 days

> Last updated (prior): 2026-05-20 — **6.9 + 6.10 + 6.11 bundled** (Phase 3 — Analytics Expansion — engine batch 2 of 2; closes the analytics computation layer at 11/22). One approval gate, four user decisions: batch shape (engine first, then UI), 6.20 disposition (audit + gap-fill, not duplicate of 5.9+5.10), forecast scope (foundation 3-of-5 + install `react-native-svg`), 6.21 IA target (Subs + Trends become tiles inside Analytics; Profile = "You"). **6.9** ships `cashflowForecast()` — equal-weighted ensemble of three models projecting end-of-current-month spend: (1) **linear-weighted** = exponentially weighted least-squares on (day_of_month, daily_total) for days 1..elapsed with weights `α^(daysElapsed - x)` (α = 0.95) and a flat-extrapolation fallback when ≤ 2 distinct daily totals exist; clamped to `>= currentSpend` so a sharp negative slope late in the month can't undercut reality; (2) **historical-month** = three-tier fallback chain — tier A = average end-of-month total for the same calendar month across prior years (excludes current month so the model never trains on its own truth), tier B = mean of last 3 complete months, tier C = lifetime average month; (3) **rolling-90d** = `(sum of last 90 live days / 90) × days_in_month` with a `min(90, history_days)` window when history is thin. Confidence band: `max/min ≤ 1.10 → high`, `≤ 1.25 → medium`, else `low`; zero-min short-circuits to `low`. Returns `{ ready, asof_month_key, days_elapsed, days_in_month, current_spend, history_days, models: { linear, historical, rolling }, ensemble, range: { min, max }, confidence }`. Gates: `{ ready: false, reason: 'no_expenses' | 'insufficient_history' | 'insufficient_month' | 'models_unresolved' }`. `approxNormalCDF()` (probability-of-overspend) deferred to 5.A.02. Cache TTL = 6h via `getCached(scope=FORECAST)`. **6.10** ships three seasonal pattern queries, all wrapped through `getCached(scope=SEASONAL, ttl=24h)`: `seasonalCalendar({months=36})` rolls expenses → (year, month) totals → averages across years per calendar-month (1..12), returns `{ cells: [{ month, avg_spend, sample_count }] × 12, max_avg }`; gates `not_enough_months` when distinct months < 12. `dayOfWeekPattern({months=12})` uses `strftime('%w', expense_date)` for 0..6 (Sun..Sat); `avg_spend` divides per-dow `SUM(amount)` by distinct dow-dates so Saturdays read as per-Saturday averages, not per-transaction; returns `{ days: [{ dow, avg_spend, txn_count, sum, day_count }] × 7, max_avg }`. `dayOfMonthHistogram({months=12})` uses `strftime('%d', ...)` for 1..31; returns `{ days: [{ dom, txn_count, sum, avg_amount }] × 31, max_txn_count, max_sum }`. All three pad missing buckets so UIs never grid-iterate with sparse arrays. **6.11** ships new module `app/src/analytics/variance.js` with `categoryVarianceMatrix({months=6})` reading `monthly_summary` LEFT JOIN `categories` (live only). Zero-pads missing months, uses Bessel-corrected (n−1) stddev across the whole window so a quiet month still affects variance, computes `cv = stddev / mean` (dimensionless — drives 6.19's heatmap intensity), tracks `max_month` / `min_month` / `max_value` / `min_value`. Categories sorted by total desc. Single-month series (window=1, theoretical) → `stddev: null, cv: null`. Soft-deleted-category rows fall to "Uncategorised" label via `COALESCE(c.name, …)`. Cache TTL = 24h via `getCached(scope=SPEND)`. **No schema migration** — every function reads existing tables. **No native rebuild** — pure JS + SQL. **No existing-file edits outside `app/src/analytics/index.js`** — Rule 9 compliant. Validation: **41/41** total — `/tmp/drift_6_9_forecast_pure.mjs` 18/18 (linear regression: flat series projects exact ×days_in_month, rising slope > flat, ≤ 2 points falls back, never below currentSpend, empty → 0, weight bias toward recent days; ensemble confidence boundaries at 1.10/1.25; zero-min → low; SQL: generated month_key, current-month sum, distinct history days, rolling-90d math, soft-delete exclusion, gates), `/tmp/drift_6_10_seasonal_validate.mjs` 10/10 (24 months × deliberately uneven distributions: July bump, Saturday bump, dom=1 bump; seasonalCalendar gates + per-month roll + 12-cell render shape; dayOfWeekPattern Sat ≫ Wed + 7-cell render; dayOfMonthHistogram dom1 > dom15 + 31-cell render; soft-delete exclusion), `/tmp/drift_6_11_variance_validate.mjs` 13/13 (4 categories × 6 months: Food volatile cv≈0.47, Bills stable cv=0, Travel single-month with zero-padding, Uncategorised id=0 sentinel, soft-deleted-category falls to label; total-desc sort; mean/stddev/max_month/min_month). **Babel parse-check passes** on all 4 touched files. **Action item for user**: `react-native-svg` install + native rebuild is **NOT** in this batch — it lands as the first step of the UI batch (6.12–6.22). TTL is rendered as absolute `expires_at` so reads are O(1) comparisons. Scope is a coarse tag (one of 8 enum values) used by the future `invalidate(scope[])` API — exposed in 6.1, called from nowhere in this batch. **6.2** scaffolds `app/src/analytics/{index,cache,spend,items,subscriptions,forecast,seasonal,lifestyle,anomaly,patterns}.js`. The four unimplemented modules (`forecast`, `seasonal`, `anomaly`, `patterns`) each export a single `notImplemented()` that throws, and are NOT re-exported from `index.js` — the public surface is exactly the 6 functions that landed. **6.3** `spendingVelocity()` does one SQL pass with a CASE-bucketed 14-day window (current 7d vs prior 7d), computes slope + pctChange, and classifies into accelerating/slowing/steady/insufficient. Thresholds: ±15% PoP, with `insufficient` fired when both windows are zero (prevents brand-new installs from being labelled "steady"). **6.8** `subscriptionLeakage()` normalises `subscriptions.period` (free-form TEXT) via `periodToMonthly()` — regex map: `mo|month→1`, `wk|week→4.345`, `yr|year|annu→1/12`, `q|quarter→1/3`, `d|day→30.42`, unknown→1 (assumed monthly). Returns `monthly_subs_total`, `monthly_spend_total` (from `monthly_summary` rollup), `leakage_ratio`, `period_breakdown` (surfaces silently-assumed-monthly subs for the 6.12 UI), and `top_subs` (3 highest monthly-equiv). Live subs only (`cancelled=0 AND deleted_at IS NULL`). **6.6** `reorderQueue()` reads `item_summary.points_count >= 3` for eligibility, pulls all live purchase_dates in one query, computes avg interval, projects `due_in_days = last_seen + avg − today`, bucketises into overdue/imminent/upcoming. **6.5** `cheapestMerchantPerItem()` is a single SQL GROUP BY (normalized_name, merchant) with `HAVING samples >= 2` (single-sample is OCR noise); returns per-item top-3 cheapest merchants by avg_unit_price. **6.4** `inflationBasket()` is the heaviest: per-item per-month avg price → top-20 items by frequency → weight each at `min(0.10, freq_share)` then renormalise → base month = first with ≥5 basket items present → per-month index = Σ(w·p/p_base) over items present in both, normalised by Σ(w_used). The 10% cap mitigates the documented "1-2 items dominate" risk (Phase 3 risk matrix). Returns `{ ready: false, reason }` shapes when data is thin (no_priced_items / no_basket / no_base_month / base_month_thin). **6.7** `lifestyleInflation()` resolves quarter ranges via SQLite `date('now','-N month')` (handles year boundaries), sums `monthly_summary` per category × quarter, computes share drift `this_share − prev_share` (chosen classifier), flags `|drift| ≥ 5pp AND prev_share ≥ 5%`. Returns both share-drift + absolute deltas so 6.15 UI can show ₹ jumps too. Migration **v25 `trips-soft-delete`** adds `deleted_at TEXT` to the only remaining mutable user-owned table that was missing it (trips was created in v1 and missed the v2 sweep) + partial index `idx_trips_alive ON trips(id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL`. `app/src/components/Toast.js` extended with optional `opts.actionLabel` + `opts.onAction` + `opts.durationMs` — back-compat: `toast('Saved')` still works. When an action is provided, banner switches to `pointerEvents='box-none'` so the inline `<TouchableOpacity>` (right-aligned `F.coral` weight-700) can receive taps; default duration bumps from 2.5s to 5s. Every `remove(id)` repo method across 9 repos now soft-deletes (`UPDATE … SET deleted_at = datetime('now') WHERE id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL`) and pairs with a new `restore(id)` (`UPDATE … SET deleted_at = NULL`). The `AND deleted_at IS NULL` guard makes remove idempotent — re-firing on an already-deleted row preserves the original timestamp. **Critical**: `expenses.remove/restore/bulkRemove/bulkRestore` cascade to `receipt_items` inside a single `withTransactionAsync` because the FK ON DELETE CASCADE only fires on real DELETE; without the cascade, an expense's items would stay alive in `item_summary` after the parent soft-deletes (validated in `/tmp/drift_2_D_09_validate.mjs:84`). Validated by **26/26 assertions** in `/tmp/drift_2_D_09_validate.mjs` (node:sqlite against v1..v25 ladder, skipping v10 transactionless): baseline rollups, expense soft-delete decrements monthly_summary, item_summary cascades to 0 via the parent cascade, restore brings both rollups back; receipt_items direct soft-delete updates item_summary; bulk variants for expenses; subs/accounts/goals/categories/trips/saved_filters all soft-hide and restore; v25 column present; idempotency of the AND-guarded UPDATE. **Babel parse-check passes across all 30 touched files**. **No native rebuild needed** — pure SQL + JS. Next app launch auto-applies v25 (single ALTER + CREATE INDEX, instant). Phase 2 totals 39/50. Overall **117/187 = 62.57%**. 2.D.07: new shared `app/src/components/SwipeableRow.js` wraps any row in a right-revealing red Delete slab via `react-native-gesture-handler/ReanimatedSwipeable` (lazy-required so a dev shell without native rebuild falls through to a plain `<View>` instead of crashing — matches 5.13's lazy-require pattern). 84px action width, snap threshold = 60% of action width, `overshootRight: false`, `friction: 2`. Action panel renders a TouchableOpacity with the 🗑 emoji + bold "Delete" label (white on F.coral); on tap it calls `swipeRef.current?.close?.()` then fires `onRightAction`. Props: `rightLabel/rightIcon/rightColor` (configurable so the same component can later host Cancel/Archive flavours), `onRightAction` (callback), `enabled` (gate — when false renders children inside a plain View, no gesture handler attached; AllExpenses uses this to suppress swipe while in 5.8's selection mode), `F` (theme). Wired into three surfaces: (1) `AllExpenses.js` row at L326 — `enabled={!selectionMode}` so swipe co-exists with long-press-multi-select; on swipe-tap fires `removeExpense(e.id)` + `toast('Deleted: <merchant>')`; row backgroundColor flipped from `'transparent'` to `F.surface` so the action panel doesn't bleed through at rest (visually identical because the parent day-group container is already F.surface). (2) `Subs.js` row at L92 — swipe fires `removeSub(s.id)` + `toast('Deleted: <name>')`; long-press Alert (Edit/Delete/Cancel) and the right-side Cancel link both preserved, so users get three paths to delete (swipe is the fast path). (3) `Detail.js` receipt-items row at L224 — swipe label customised to "Remove"; calls new `items.remove(id)` repo method (added at items/repo.js L194 — hard DELETE, matches 5.8's bulkRemove decision in Decision log entry 624) exposed through `useItemActions().remove`, then local `setItems((prev) => prev.filter(x => x.id !== it.id))` so the row vanishes without a full re-fetch; `toast('Removed: <name>')`. Receipt-item rows now have `backgroundColor: F.surface` for the same swipe-reveal reason. The existing AD trigger on `receipt_items` (v12) handles item_summary + item_fts rollup maintenance automatically — no new trigger work. Parent `expenses.amount` is intentionally NOT recomputed (decision logged below — receipt total reflects what the user actually paid, items are itemisation detail). All three swipe handlers wrap the repo call in try/catch with `logError(...)` + `Alert.alert('Delete failed', ...)` on rejection. 2.D.08: scope confirmed identical to 5.8's already-shipped "Batch select mode on AllExpenses (long-press, action bar, batch recategorize/delete/export)" — execution_roadmap.md L606 explicitly describes 5.8 as covering long-press multi-select; tracker line at task_tracker.md L226 is the leaf for the same behaviour. Closed as `(= 5.8)` cross-reference, zero new code. Phase 2 totals 38/50. Overall **116/187 = 62.03%**. **2.D.05** Mood picker collapsible/optional shipped. `MoodPicker` primitive extended with `selected: boolean` + `onClear?: () => void` (existing `value`/`onChange`/`F` props unchanged — back-compat for any callers that still pass `selected=true` by default). When `selected=false`, the component renders a single ＋ Add mood pill (self-aligning, F.surface bg + F.line border, F.ink2 label) instead of the 5-face row + label. When `selected=true` it renders today's row of 5 emoji touchables with the matching face label, plus a small "Clear" link in the top-right corner whenever `onClear` is supplied. `Add.js` splits the old `[mood, setMood] = useState(1)` into `[moodIdx, setMoodIdx] = useState(1)` (which face is selected when expanded) + `[moodOn, setMoodOn] = useState(false)` (whether the user has engaged at all). Card body now wires `selected={moodOn}` + `onChange={(i) => { setMoodIdx(i); setMoodOn(true); }}` (first tap on a face simultaneously selects it and flips the engagement gate) + `onClear={moodOn ? () => setMoodOn(false) : undefined}` (clear only available once engaged; setting it undefined when off keeps the chip-state from showing a vestigial Clear link). Both quick-mode and detailed-mode save paths (Add.js L259 + L290) now write `mood: moodOn ? MOODS[moodIdx] : null` — every fresh expense the user logs without touching the picker stores NULL instead of the legacy silent 😌 default. `EditExpense.js` applies the same dual-state pattern with `moodOn = !!e?.mood` seeded from the existing row — moded rows open expanded with their stored face selected and a Clear option, unmoded rows open as the chip. Both `updateExpense` and `updateExpenseWithItems` callsites (L67 + L80) write the same `moodOn ? MOODS[moodIdx] : null`. The legacy `initialMoodIdx = e?.mood ? MOODS.indexOf(e.mood) : 1` line is intentionally untouched — it still produces a sensible index for when the user does expand, and `moodOn` independently gates whether that index ever reaches the DB. `Scan.js` L318 hard-coded `mood: '😌'` flipped to `mood: null` so OCR-saved expenses no longer get a silent mood stamp the user never asked for; no UI added to the Scan review screen (would be a new affordance, deferred). **No schema migration** — `expenses.mood TEXT` is already nullable at v1, and `expRepo.create` already coerces `undefined → null` via `mood ?? null` at repo.js L81. All readers verified null-safe: `Home.js:299` and `AllExpenses.js:339` use `r.mood ? ' ${r.mood}' : ''`, `Detail.js:120` wraps in `e.mood && (...)`, `PotDetail.js:127` same, `filters.js:157` gates the IN-list on `c.moods.length > 0`, `export.js:59` uses `e.mood || ''`. Phase 2 totals 36/50. Overall **114/187 = 60.96%**. 5.13 + 5.14 + 5.15 shipped in one batch. 5.13 introduces `app/src/features/expenses/components/ReceiptViewer.js` — a full-screen transparent `Modal` (rgba(0,0,0,0.92) backdrop, fade animation, `GestureHandlerRootView` already present at the app root) hosting a hand-rolled pinch/zoom canvas composed via `Gesture.Simultaneous(pinch, Gesture.Race(doubleTap, pan))` on top of `Animated.View` from react-native-reanimated 4.1. Scale bounded `[1, 4]` with snap-back to 1× when released below 1.05; pan only active when scale > 1.05 and clamped against `(scale-1) * dim / 2` per-axis so the image can't be dragged off-canvas; double-tap toggles 1× ↔ 2×. All RNGH/Reanimated imports lazy-required so a dev shell missing the native rebuild gracefully falls through to a static contain-fit `Image` (the rest of the UI keeps working). Detail.js gains a new "Receipt" card between the Tax-invoice card and the Items list when `e.receipt_path || e.receipt_uri` is set — 64×88 thumb (cover fit, F.cream placeholder + 🧾 emoji when no thumb URI) with "Tap to view · Pinch to zoom · drag to pan" hint and a chevron; tap opens the viewer. 5.15 ships the reader flip and lazy-migrate trigger. New shared pure-JS module `app/src/features/expenses/receiptUri.js` exports `pickReceiptUri(e) → {full, thumb}` (prefers permanent `receipt_path` / `receipt_thumb`, falls back to legacy `receipt_uri` so legacy rows still render), `hasReceipt(e)`, and `needsMigration(e)` (true iff `receipt_uri` set AND no `receipt_path` — the exact shape lazy-migrate acts on). New repo method `expenses.attachReceiptStorage(id, {path, thumb, bytes})` UPDATEs the v7 columns in-place — leaves `receipt_uri` intact as a safety net (see Decision log). Lazy-migrate hook lives inside `ReceiptViewer.js`'s open-side `useEffect`: when `needsMigration(row)` returns true, fires `persistReceipt(row.receipt_uri)` → `expRepo.attachReceiptStorage(...)`, surfaces a "Saving permanently…" inline pill at the top while running, calls `onMigrated` to bubble the new URIs into Detail's local row-shadow state so the thumb refreshes without a provider hop. `filters.js`'s `hasReceipt` predicate flipped from `e.receipt_uri IS [NOT] NULL` to `COALESCE(e.receipt_path, e.receipt_uri) IS [NOT] NULL` so saved-filter rows match both legacy and migrated rows uniformly. 5.14 adds the predictive "Last time ₹X" chip to Add.js's merchant input. New state `lastSpend` + `chipApplied` + `lookupLastSpend(merchantId)` calling `expRepo.lastAtMerchant(merchantId)` (already added by 5.9). Two trigger points reuse the existing 5.9/5.10 wiring: `pickSuggestion(s)` (autocomplete tap — knows merchant_id directly) and `applyAliasIfAny(name, merchantId)` (free-typed merchant that resolved via bundle/user alias — chip fires when `hit.merchantId != null`). Inline `LastSpendChip` sub-component renders below the merchant input in BOTH Quick (hero card) and Detailed (MERCHANT field) layouts: F.cream pill `🕒 Last time: ₹420 · 12 May`. Tap calls `formatAmountForKeypad(lastSpend.amount)` (`X.YY` two-decimal format matching the keypad's pad-and-shift reducer) and sets `chipApplied=true` (chip flips coral with ✓); second tap clears the amount back to `'0.00'` and resets `chipApplied`. A manual keypad press cancels `chipApplied` (so a subsequent chip tap re-fills) but leaves the typed value alone. Visibility predicate: `!isIncome && !!lastSpend && (parseFloat(amount) === 0 || chipApplied)` — typing past the chip hides it, but the applied-then-edit state keeps the chip visible while the user tweaks. Merchant text changing through `onMerchantChange` clears `lastSpend` + `chipApplied` so a new merchant rehydrates from scratch. New exported helpers `formatAmountForKeypad(n)` (clamps to `'0.00'` on NaN/negative/Infinity/string/null/0; `toFixed(2)` otherwise) and `formatChipDate(iso)` (YYYY-MM-DD → "12 May"). Validated by 3 standalone Node scripts: **63/63 total** — `/tmp/drift_5_13_pickreceipt_pure.mjs` 21/21 (all 8 (path/thumb/uri) combinations + null safety + hasReceipt incl. thumb-only=false + needsMigration including empty-uri=false), `/tmp/drift_5_14_chip_pure.mjs` 28/28 (formatAmountForKeypad across 11 inputs incl. Infinity/NaN/null/negative/string; formatChipDate across 8 incl. malformed; tap-state machine 4-step trace incl. keypad-cancel; visibility predicate 5 cases incl. income mode + applied-then-edit), `/tmp/drift_5_15_migrate_validate.mjs` 14/14 via `node:sqlite` (4-row corpus: legacy/migrated/no-receipt/soft-deleted-legacy; pre-migrate hasReceipt=true matches legacy+migrated, hasReceipt=false matches no-receipt, soft-delete excluded; attachReceiptStorage stamps path/thumb/bytes while retaining receipt_uri; idempotent re-attach overwrites; null-path no-op; null-thumb permitted; null/missing id no-op). Babel parse-check across all 6 touched files passes via `@babel/parser`. **No user action needed** — pure JS + UI, no schema change (v7 already covered the columns), no new dependencies (RNGH + Reanimated already installed), no native rebuild. Phase 2.A now 14/15 (only 5.1 remains — it's marked done-in-Phase-3 as the `payment_method` migration which 5.4 closed implicitly). Phase 2 totals 35/50. Overall **113/187 = 60.4%**.
> Last updated (prior): 2026-05-19 — Phase 2.A advanced again: **5.11 + 5.12** shipped in one batch. 5.11 surfaces the GST data already persisted by 4.8/4.17 as a new "Tax invoice" card on `Detail.js` (between the meta-table and the items list). Pure-JS helper `buildTaxRows(e, sym)` extracted inline in Detail.js (testable without React): renders `Subtotal · CGST(rate%) · SGST(rate%) · IGST(rate%) · Tax total · GSTIN · Invoice #` rows, each conditional on the underlying field being set. Effective-rate suffix (`(2.5%)`, `(9.0%)` etc.) computed from `cgst / subtotal * 100` and shown only when `subtotal > 0` so dodgy OCR data (amount ≤ Σtax) collapses to a clean components-only view. Subtotal row hidden when negative/zero. Tax-total row carries `emphasis: true` (cream background, weight 600) to anchor the breakdown. GSTIN row uses `fontVariant: ['tabular-nums']` + 0.5px letter-spacing for the 15-char code. No schema change, no repo change, no persistence change — every wire was already laid by 4.8 (v16 columns + Scan.js threading) and 4.17 (v17 per-item GST rates). 5.12 ships the receipt image pipeline foundation: new module `app/src/features/scan/receiptStorage.js` exports `persistReceipt(srcUri) → {path, thumb, bytes} | null`. Pipeline: lazy-requires `expo-file-system/legacy` + `expo-image-manipulator`, ensures `documentDirectory/drift/receipts/{full,thumb}/` exist (idempotent — skips makeDirectoryAsync when getInfoAsync reports exists:true), generates a UUID via `crypto.randomUUID()` with `Date.now()+Math.random()` fallback, runs `manipulateAsync(uri, [resize:{width:1600}], {compress:0.95, format:JPEG})` for full + `[resize:{width:320}], {compress:0.7}` for thumb (JPEG re-encode is the EXIF strip — manipulator constructs new bytes from decoded RGBA so the original EXIF block doesn't carry through), moves the cacheDirectory outputs into `full/<uuid>.jpg` and `thumb/<uuid>.jpg` via `moveAsync` (with `copyAsync + deleteAsync(idempotent:true)` fallback on EXDEV-style cross-volume errors). Returns `null` on any failure (require failure, manipulator throw, FS write error, missing srcUri) so the caller falls back to legacy-only save. `expenses.createWithItems` INSERT extended with `receipt_path, receipt_thumb, receipt_bytes` columns (v7 already had them — no migration needed). `Scan.js performSave` calls `persistReceipt(expense.receipt_uri)` before the DB write, merges `{receipt_path, receipt_thumb, receipt_bytes}` into the expense payload when non-null. Legacy `receipt_uri` continues to be written alongside per chosen option D — 5.15 owns the reader flip and the lazy-migrate of existing rows. `expenses.create()` and `expenses.update()` left untouched (Rule 9 — manual Add has no receipt UI; EditExpense receipt-attach is out of scope). Validated by 3 standalone Node scripts: **114/114 total** — `drift_5_11_detail_pure.mjs` 32/32 (15 cases: gstin-only / cgst+sgst at 2.5+2.5% / igst-only at 5% / mixed all three / amount<Σtax hides subtotal AND suppresses rates / amount=Σtax hides subtotal / invoice-only / all-zeros+empty-meta=null / whitespace-only gstin=null / null expense / 9% half-rate / USD symbol / igst-no-gstin / string-typed numerics coerced), `drift_5_12_persist_pure.mjs` 35/35 (Module._load patched to inject FS+manipulator stubs; 6 scenarios: happy path with both subdirs created + 2 manipulator calls at the right widths/quality + matching uuid full/thumb + bytes from getInfoAsync; EXDEV fallback fires copyAsync×2+deleteAsync×2 with idempotent:true; manipulator throw → null; null/empty/undefined srcUri → null; getInfoAsync missing size → bytes=0; dirs already exist → makeDirectoryAsync skipped), `drift_5_12_repo_validate.mjs` 47/47 via `node:sqlite` (applies all 24 migrations from schema.js directly; confirms expenses.{receipt_path, receipt_thumb, receipt_bytes, gstin, invoice_number, cgst, sgst, igst, payment_method} all present; INSERT with all 21 columns round-trips correctly; null-case insert preserves receipt_uri alone). Babel parse-check across all 4 touched files passes via `@babel/parser`. **No user action needed** — pure JS + SQL + UI, no migration, no new deps, no native rebuild. Phase 2.A now 11/15 (5.2, 5.3, 5.4, 5.5, 5.6, 5.7, 5.8, 5.9, 5.10, 5.11, 5.12 done; 4 leaf tasks remain: 5.13, 5.14, 5.15). Phase 2 totals 32/50.
> Last updated (prior): 2026-05-19 — Phase 2.A advanced again: **5.9 + 5.10** shipped in one batch. 5.10 ships migration v24 (`merchant-aliases`: id PK, `alias_key TEXT NOT NULL`, `merchant_id` nullable FK merchants SET NULL, `category_id` nullable FK categories SET NULL, `source` CHECK ('user'|'bundle'), `created_at/updated_at` DEFAULTS, `deleted_at` reserved for future Recycle Bin) + `UNIQUE INDEX idx_merchant_aliases_key ON (alias_key, source) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL` so a soft-deleted row can co-exist with a fresh user choice + `idx_merchant_aliases_merchant ON (merchant_id) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL`. New bundled `app/src/features/expenses/merchantMap.json` ships **58 popular Indian merchants** (scaffold per the chosen option — full ~1k corpus deferred to a future data-fill task that won't need code review): 11 Food & Drink (Swiggy, Zomato, Domino's, McD, KFC, Starbucks, CCD), 10 Groceries (Swiggy Instamart, Blinkit, Zepto, BigBasket, DMart, Reliance Fresh/Smart, More Retail, Amul, Mother Dairy), 11 Transport (Uber, Ola, Rapido, Namma Yatri, IRCTC, IndiGo, Vistara, Air India, HP/IOC/BPCL), 7 Health (Apollo Pharmacy, MedPlus, Tata 1mg, Netmeds, PharmEasy, Cult.fit), 5 Subscriptions (Netflix, Hotstar, Prime Video, Spotify, YouTube Premium), 5 Bills (Jio, Airtel, Vi, BSNL, Tata Power), 7 Fun (Amazon, Flipkart, Myntra, AJIO, BookMyShow, PVR, INOX). All keys are pre-canonicalised via `lightNormMerchant()` (lowercased, suffix-stripped); category strings match `STARTER_CATEGORIES.name` exactly so case-insensitive lookup works against user-renamed pots too. New pure-JS module `app/src/features/expenses/aliases.repo.js` exports the `aliases` singleton with `lookup(text, {categories}) → {categoryId, merchantId?, source} | null`, `recordUserChoice({alias, merchantId, categoryId})` (UPSERT, source='user'), `clearUserAliases()` (soft-delete all user rows — reserved for a future "forget merchants" affordance), `list({source?})`, plus exported `lookupBundle(aliasKey)` for the validation harness. Two-tier lookup: user-source rows beat bundle every time; bundle's category string maps to the user's `categories` array case-insensitively, returns null if no user category matches. Substring matching via longest-key-first sorted iteration so `"Swiggy Instamart Mumbai"` correctly hits `swiggy instamart` (Groceries) before falling through to `swiggy` (Food). 5.9 extends `merchants.repo.js` with `suggest(prefix, {limit=5}) → [{id, name, canonical_name, score}]` (two-pass: exact + prefix + contains via indexed LIKE first, ranked by length asc; JW ≥ 0.78 fallback only when SQL underflows the limit) + `resolveExisting(name)` (resolve but never INSERT — used by autocomplete to attach a merchant_id without creating ghost rows). `expenses.repo.js` gains 5 new merchant-intelligence queries: `topMerchants({months, limit})` (SUM/COUNT/MAX/min group ranked by spend, gated on `e.merchant_id IS NOT NULL` and a windowed `month_key >= strftime('%Y-%m','now','-N months')` — uses the indexed `month_key`), `merchantSummary({merchantId, months})` (returns total_all/total_window/txn_count_all/avg/first_seen/last_seen in a single CASE-WHEN aggregate so the screen header reads one row), `merchantMonthlyTrend({merchantId, months})` (12-month bar chart data — joined directly against `expenses` because `monthly_summary` aggregates by category not merchant), `merchantCategoryBreakdown({merchantId, months})` (one row per category — LEFT JOIN keeps the uncategorised bucket as `id=NULL`), `merchantRecents({merchantId, limit})` (recent spends list with category join for the row UI). Plus `lastAtMerchant(merchantId)` for the future 5.14 "Last time ₹X" chip (single-row LIMIT 1 of the most-recent live row). All five queries soft-delete-aware via `NOT_DELETED_E`. `expenses.create()` now resolves `merchant_id` (was previously only `createWithItems` — manual Add path was leaving the FK NULL so MerchantDetail rollups missed quick-spend rows); `expenses.update()` re-resolves when merchant text changes (was leaving a stale FK after EditExpense merchant edits). New `MerchantDetail.js` screen mirrors `ItemTrend` structurally — hero card (name + window total + visits + avg + last_seen), 12-month tappable bar chart with selected-bar callout, category breakdown strip with per-row share bar + percentage, recent spends list tapping into `Detail`. New `Merchants.js` leaderboard with 4-range pill (3mo/6mo/12mo/All), pull-to-refresh, 1-50 rank list rendered into a single card. Both registered in `navigation/index.js` (Stack screens; MerchantDetail title threads `route.params.displayName`). `Detail.js` makes the merchant text tappable (dotted underline) only when `merchant_id` is set so legacy NULL-id rows stay non-interactive. `Profile.js` gains a "🏪 Top merchants" row in the Items & Trends section. `Add.js` rewires the merchant TextInput: `onMerchantChange` (180ms debounce) calls `merchantRepo.suggest`, renders a 5-row dropdown below the input (covers both Quick hero-card and Detailed merchant-field layouts). Tapping a suggestion fills the name + pins `pickedMerchantIdRef`; both that path and `onBlur` (free-typed merchants that didn't pick a suggestion) trigger `aliases.lookup` → silent auto-switch of `potId` ONLY when `userTouchedCategoryRef` is false (any prior pill tap permanently locks the user's manual choice for the session). Save path threads the picked merchant_id through and fires `aliases.recordUserChoice` after success so the next visit reinforces the mapping. `ExpensesProvider.addExpense/addExpenseWithItems` now return the saved row (additive — every caller was discarding the return value previously). Validated by 3 standalone Node scripts: **80/80 total** — `/tmp/drift_5_9_5_10_validate.mjs` 38/38 via `node:sqlite` (v1..v24 ladder; merchant_aliases column shape + UNIQUE partial-index behaviour: user/bundle coexist, duplicate-user rejected, soft-delete-then-reinsert allowed with tombstone retained; CHECK rejects bogus source; FK ON DELETE SET NULL on merchant removal; topMerchants ranking + 6/12-mo window correctness; merchantSummary CASE-WHEN sums; merchantMonthlyTrend ordering + window; merchantCategoryBreakdown including NULL category bucket; merchantRecents ordering + soft-delete exclusion + LIMIT clamp; lastAtMerchant most-recent semantics), `/tmp/drift_5_10_aliases_pure.mjs` 28/28 (bundle hit exact + Pvt-Ltd canonicalisation + longest-key substring; bundle miss; case-insensitive category mapping; user-row-wins-over-bundle + NULL-user-falls-through-to-bundle; soft-deleted-user-falls-through; lightNormMerchant edge cases; merchantMap.json hygiene — every key in canonical form + every category exists in STARTER + 58 entries + sort determinism), `/tmp/drift_5_9_suggest_pure.mjs` 14/14 against node:sqlite (exact > prefix > contains ranking; length-asc tiebreaker; JW fallback for typo "zoomato"; blank/whitespace/null safety; limit clamp; Pvt-Ltd suffix folds into canonical exact-match). Babel parse-check across all 12 touched files passes. **No user action needed** — pure SQL + JS + UI; next app launch auto-applies v24. Phase 2.A now 9/15 (5.2, 5.3, 5.4, 5.5, 5.6, 5.7, 5.8, 5.9, 5.10 done; 6 leaf tasks remain). Phase 2 totals 30/50.

Profile "More" section gains a "📤 Export your data" Row pointing at the modal. 5.8 adds `expenses.bulkRemove(ids)` + `expenses.bulkUpdateCategory(ids, categoryId)` to the repo — both wrap the writes in a single `withTransactionAsync` and chunk at 500 ids to stay under SQLITE_MAX_VARIABLE_NUMBER. `ExpensesProvider` exposes `bulkRemoveExpenses` + `bulkRecategorizeExpenses` that call into the repo then full-refresh (SQL `expenses.list` + `summaryByCategory`) rather than optimistic patching, because the row's joined category columns need re-fetching after a recat. `AllExpenses.js` gains a `selectedIds: Set` state (mode derived from size > 0), long-press to enter mode + tap-to-toggle in mode, header-right swap (search 🔍 ↔ "Done"), Android `BackHandler` to exit on hardware back, a fixed bottom action bar with three buttons (Recategorize / Export / Delete — destructive colour on the last) that disappears when selection clears, plus a 500-id soft cap (no spurious re-render when blocked). Row visual when selected: coral 4px left border + ✓ overlay on the emoji square + cream background. New component `app/src/features/expenses/components/CategoryPickerSheet.js` (bottom Modal, Pressable backdrop dismisses, lists pots + "Clear category" affordance) drives the recat flow. > Last updated (prior): 2026-05-19 — Phase 2.C closed (apart from the explicitly-deferred 4.20): **4.24** (multi-image receipt stitching) shipped. New pure-JS module `app/src/ocr/stitchPages.js` exports `mergePages(pages)` — accepts an array of `parseReceipt()` outputs in capture order, returns a single merged parsed shape. Item dedup at fixed PRICE_EPS=0.01 by (normalized_name + |price diff|); first-occurrence wins for identity. Each output item annotated with `_pageId` (page index) + `_origIdx` (position within that page) so the Scan UI can preserve user edits across re-merges. Total reconciliation: walks pages in REVERSE, takes the first non-zero total found (last-page-grand-total wins), else falls back to sum-of-merged-items. Headers (merchant / date / gstin / orderId / invoiceNumber / format / formatLabel / brand): picked from the page with the highest `formatConfidence` whose field is non-empty (and not the parser's `Unknown store` / fallback-date sentinels). Bill-level amounts (cgst / sgst / igst / subtotal / tax): same picker, ignores zero/null values. Fees + discounts: concat (no dedup — typically unique per page). Confidence: re-scored via `scoreConfidence` on the merged shape (per-page confidences don't compose linearly). `bands` + `columns` (4.22 template capture material): last-non-null wins so the latest page's template snapshot survives. New `ScanService.scanAndProcessMore(uri, priorPages, pots)` runs the full single-page pipeline on a new image (lightPreprocess → ML Kit → optional Tesseract fallback → optional template apply per 4.21/4.22), merges with priorPages, and returns a review payload carrying `_pages`, `_pageCount`, `_parsed`, `_parsedForTemplate`. `scanAndProcess` now also attaches `_parsed` (full parsed shape) so single-page callers and the stitching path share one snapshot format. Scan.js gains `pagesRef = useRef([])` + `pageCount` state + `addingPage` in-flight flag + `addAnotherPage` / `addPageFromPicker(source)` handlers; `processImage` seeds `pagesRef = [_parsed]` and tags first-page items with `_pageId=0, _origIdx=i`; the review screen renders a new "Add page N+1 · for long receipts" pill above the Retake/Save row that prompts Camera/Gallery → runs `scanAndProcessMore` → merges with edit-preservation (`Map<pageId:origIdx, item>` lookup; user-edited rows survive; manually-added rows with no `_pageId` survive too at the end). Header preservation: merchant/date/total/taxInvoice overwritten only when current value equals the prior-merged value (skip-if-user-edited). `resetScreen` clears `pagesRef`, `pageCount`, `addingPage`. UX: single toast "Page N added" on success; Alert "Could not add page" on failure (never blocks the save flow). Validated by 2 standalone Node scripts: **78/78 total** — 41/41 `mergePages` pure-JS logic (null safety, single-page identity, 3-page overlap dedup with annotation correctness, last-page total wins, sum fallback, PRICE_EPS at boundary, header pick by formatConfidence + Unknown-store sentinel skip, GSTIN/invoice non-null win, per-component tax/subtotal picking, fees/discounts concat, format/formatConfidence max, 3-page DMart simulation 10 items + grand-total wins, bands/columns last-non-null), 37/37 structural integration (stitchPages exports, ScanService scanAndProcessMore wiring + _parsed attachment, Scan.js state declarations + handlers + first-page seeding + edit-preservation map + UI button label/disabled state + resetScreen extension). **No user action needed** — pure JS + UI, no schema, no native, no deps. Phase 2.B unchanged at 8/13; Phase 2.C now **4/5** (4.21, 4.22, 4.23, 4.24 done; only **4.20** [-] deferred); Phase 2 totals now 21/50.
> Last updated (prior): 2026-05-19 — Phase 2.C advanced again: **4.23** (pharmacy-specific extraction strategy) shipped. Migration v19 adds three nullable TEXT columns to `receipt_items`: `batch_no`, `expiry_date`, `mfg_date` (no indexes — no query plans on these yet; future expiry-reminder feature 7.x can add its own index). Three new keyword-anchored regexes in patterns.js: `BATCH_RE` (matches `Batch`/`B.No`/`BN`/`Lot No` + alphanumeric token incl. `/` `-`, 3..18 chars), `EXPIRY_RE` (matches `EXP`/`EXPIRY`/`Exp Dt`/`Exp.` + date), `MFG_RE` (matches `MFG`/`M.Dt`/`Mfd` + date) — all keyword-anchored so a bare date never accidentally becomes an expiry. New `extractItemsPharmacy(rows, bands)` strategy mirrors extractItemsTabular with two pharmacy-aware additions: (1) before matchAmounts, scans the current row + 3 rows back (`PHARMACY_LOOKBACK = 3`) for batch/expiry/mfg and strips the same-row matches from the working text so `B/24/011` no longer leaks `/011` into the amount list, (2) buildItem receives `batchNo`/`expiryDate`/`mfgDate` and persists them on the RawItem. New `parseDrugDate(raw, mode)` helper accepts MM/YY, MM/YYYY, DD/MM/YY, DD/MM/YYYY across `/`, `-`, `.` separators; `normaliseExpiry` uses `last`-of-month for month/year-only inputs (semantic: "valid until end of month"); `normaliseMfg` uses `first`-of-month (semantic: "manufactured at start of month"). 2-digit year window: 00..79 → 2000..2079, 80..99 → 1980..1999. `FORMAT_CONFIGS.pharmacy.itemStrategy` flipped from `tabular` → `pharmacy`; auto-activation on every receipt the existing FORMAT_SIGNATURES tag as pharmacy (Apollo Pharmacy / MedPlus / 1mg / Netmeds / PharmEasy brand hints + GSTIN). `dispatchItems` gains `case 'pharmacy'`. Both `expenses.createWithItems` and `items.replaceItems` INSERT statements extended with the 3 new columns. Out of scope: drug strength (500mg) into a dedicated column, drug form (tab/cap/syp) extraction, schedule (H/H1/X) classification, Detail-screen UI surfacing (defer to 5.11), expiry-reminder notifications (defer to 7.x). NULL persistence for non-pharmacy items), 48/48 patterns + normalisers (7 BATCH_RE positive + 4 negative; 5 EXPIRY_RE positive + 3 negative; 4 MFG_RE positive + 2 negative; 16 normaliseExpiry edge cases incl. leap-year 02/24, month/year-only last-of-month, 4-part rejection, year out-of-band; 3 normaliseMfg first-of-month; 4 two-digit year window edge cases), 30/30 structural integration (patterns exports, parseReceipt imports + helpers + PHARMACY_LOOKBACK, extractItemsPharmacy + dispatch + name-strip + buildItem extension, FORMAT_CONFIGS.pharmacy.itemStrategy flip with restaurant/departmental unaffected, both repo INSERT extensions). **No user action needed** — pure SQL + JS; next app launch auto-applies v19. Custom Kotlin `TesseractModule` (Tesseract4Android 4.7.0 via jitpack) wraps Tesseract 5 LSTM as the Tier-2 OCR engine; the abandoned `react-native-tesseract-ocr@2.0.3` (last published 2022) was rejected as the integration path per Rule 1. Gradle: `com.github.adaptech-cz.Tesseract4Android:4.7.0` added (jitpack already in root repos). ProGuard: keep rules for `com.googlecode.{tesseract,leptonica}.android.**`. ScanService.scanAndProcess attaches `_ocr` so Scan.js can stash the OCR+parsed snapshot in a ref and call `writeCandidate` fire-and-forget after save (the candidate JSON contains structured OCR text only — no images); Profile screen gained a "Help improve scans" section with Auto-capture toggle (default ON), "Export receipts for parser improvement" button (lazy-requires expo-sharing — surfaces a clear path-only fallback when dep is missing), and a conditional "Clear captured" row. 4.20 marked deferred with a Decision-log entry — without 4.19's corpus there is no way to measure whether native preprocess actually improves accuracy. Validated by 5 standalone Node scripts: **78/78 total** — 24/24 reconcile-tolerance, 16/16 GST regex+disambig, 15/15 v17 migration ladder + round-trip, 9/9 golden runner across oracle/broken/missing/tolerant/error stubs, 14/14 capture selection + edited-delta. **Action item for user:** `cd app && npx expo install expo-sharing` to enable the Export button's share-sheet UX (without it, the button still works but only reports the bundle path via Alert). Phase 1.D unchanged at 8/8; Phase 2.B now 8/13 (4.17, 4.18 done; 4.19 [!] PARTIAL).
> Last updated (prior): 2026-05-19 — Phase 1.D fully closed + Phase 2.B opened: **4.9, 4.10, 4.11, 4.12, 4.13, 4.14, 4.15, 4.16** landed in one batch (8 tasks, 5 new files + 5 file edits, no migration — v7 receipt-hash columns + v4 merchants table reused). 4.9 upgraded `@react-native-ml-kit/text-recognition` v1.5.2 → v2.0.0; `recognize()` now uses the v2 `(uri, script=LATIN)` signature and accepts an optional `{ script }` for future Hindi-toggle work (default stays Latin per user-selected option). 4.10 added per-line mean-of-elements `confidence` + per-element `{x, width}` to each line; values default to 1.0 when the native bridge doesn't surface ML Kit's `Text.Element.getConfidence()` (which the current upstream v2 bridge doesn't — defensive read so we're correct when it does). 4.11 installed `expo-image-manipulator@~14.0.8`; new `app/src/ocr/preprocess.js` exports `lightPreprocess(uri)` (resize long-edge → 1600px, re-save PNG, failure-tolerant fallthrough) wired into `scanAndProcess` before `recognize`. 4.12 new `app/src/ocr/columns.js` exports `detectColumns(rows) → {columns, gapWidths}` (5px bucket histogram, 30px min gap) and `tokenColumn(columns, token)`; `mergeIntoRows` now retains per-token `{text, x, width}` in `row.tokens` so detectColumns has positions to work with. 4.13 new `extractItemsColumnar` strategy buckets tokens into name/qty/rate/amount columns; the `tabular` dispatcher arm now tries columnar first and falls back to existing extractor when columns < 3 or no gap qualifies (auto-fallback option). 4.14 new `app/src/ocr/fingerprint.js` exports `fingerprintReceipt(parsed)` (djb2 of merchant|date|total|item-count|sorted-prices) + `softFingerprint(parsed)` (merchant|round(total)); `expenses.findDuplicate({hash, softHash, date})` returns `{kind: 'exact'|'soft', expense}|null` with a ±1-day window for the soft side; `createWithItems` persists both hashes via the v7 columns; Scan.js recomputes hashes from the in-memory parsed shape (so user edits invalidate the OCR-time hash), gates `save()` on a dedup pre-check, and opens a Modal with Cancel/Keep-both when a duplicate is found. 4.15 new `app/src/core/utils/strings.js` exports `jaroWinkler(a, b)` (standard implementation, prefix cap 4, scaling 0.1) + `lightNormMerchant(name)` (lowercase, trim, strip trailing pvt-ltd/private-limited/llp/inc/co); new `app/src/features/expenses/merchants.repo.js` exports `merchants.resolve(name) → merchant_id` (NOCASE exact → JW ≥ 0.92 → INSERT), called from `createWithItems` inside the transaction. 4.16 new `extractFuelItem(rows, bands)` parses both inline (`12.345 L x 105.50 = 1302.40`) and split-row (`Quantity:…/Rate:…/Amount:…`) fuel layouts; fuel-type detected from `/petrol|diesel|cng|gasoline/i` (defaults to `Fuel`); single item emitted with `kind: 'fuel'`, `unit: 'l'`; `FORMAT_CONFIGS.fuel.itemStrategy` flipped from `totals-only` to `fuel`. Validated by 5 standalone Node scripts: jaroWinkler+lightNorm 20/20, detectColumns 11/11, fingerprint 13/13, fuel extraction 18/18, merchant-resolve+findDuplicate (node:sqlite) 14/14 — **76/76 total**. Phase 1.D now 8/8 (complete); Phase 2.B now 6/13.
> Last updated (prior): 2026-05-19 — Phase 1.D OCR closed: **4.2, 4.5, 4.6, 4.7, 4.8** landed in one batch. 4.5 added `core/domain/date.js` with `validateDateParts(d,m,y) → bool` (real-calendar check + plausibility window [2000, currentYear+1]) and `normaliseDateParts`; `parseReceipt.findDate()` rewritten to walk every match and skip invalids before falling through. 4.6 renamed `classifyRow` → `classifyRowWithContext(text, amounts?)`; price-position rule requires the matched amount to sit in the last 30% of the line or be the only amount on the row — mid-line prices on lines that incidentally mention "total/tax" fall through to `item`. 4.7 new `deriveQtyFromRate(amount, rate)` helper snaps to integers 1..99 plus {0.25, 0.5, 0.75} within 5% relative tolerance; `extractItemsTabular` now uses it (replaces the inline integer-only Math.abs<0.05 check). 4.2 bundled `core/domain/product_synonyms.json` (~150 Hinglish→English staples, ~5KB) + `core/domain/hindi_product_map.json` (~95 Devanagari→English entries, ~5KB); new `core/domain/synonyms.js` `canonicalizeName(s)` chains Hindi→Latin lookups; `normalizeName()` calls it whole-string before singularising — `दूध / Doodh / Milk` all collapse to `milk`, while `Amul Doodh` deliberately stays uncollapsed (brand preserved). 4.8 added migration v16 (`tax-invoice-fields`): `expenses` gains `gstin/invoice_number/cgst/sgst/igst`, `receipt_items` gains `hsn`; new `CGST_AMOUNT_RE`/`SGST_AMOUNT_RE`/`IGST_AMOUNT_RE` regexes split the previously-summed `tax`; `HSN_RE` upgraded with a capture group for per-row item extraction; `INVOICE_NO_RE` introduced separately from `ORDER_ID_RE`; ScanService → Scan.js → `expRepo.createWithItems` + `itemRepo.replaceItems` thread the new fields through (manual Add/EditExpense paths intentionally untouched — no UI surface this round; Detail UI is 5.11). Validated by 4 standalone node tests: 9/9 date, 12/12 row-classify, 14/15 qty-snap (1 mis-specified expectation in the test, behaviour correct), 12/12 canonicaliser, 9/9 tax regexes, 13/13 v16 migration ladder + round-trip. Phase 1.D now 6/8 (4.9–4.10 remain in Phase 1.D; 4.11+ live in Phase 2.B).
> Last updated (prior): 2026-05-18 — 3.11 → 3.19 + 1.C.20 closed in one schema+rewrite batch (migrations v12…v15 + 1.C.20 sweep). 4 new migration objects appended to `app/src/db/schema.js`: v12 adds rollup tables `monthly_summary (month_key, category_id, total, txn_count)` + `item_summary (normalized_name, …, points_count)` plus 6 maintenance triggers; v13 adds `expense_fts` (FTS5 over merchant + notes) + 3 sync triggers; v14 adds `item_fts` (FTS5 over name + normalized_name) + 3 sync triggers; v15 backfills all four artefacts from live rows. All AI/AD triggers gate on `deleted_at IS NULL`; AU triggers do `-OLD (if live) + NEW (if live)` so soft-delete flips, category moves, month-boundary moves, and normalized_name renames all maintain rollups correctly. Repo rewrites: `expenses.summaryByCategory` and `monthlyTrend` now read from `monthly_summary`; `items.trackedItems` and `items.suggest` now read from `item_summary` (eliminates 4 correlated subqueries); `trips.listWithCategories` collapsed from N+1 to single LEFT JOIN; 4 `substr(date,1,7)` sites swapped for `month_key`. New `app/src/db/predicates.js` exports `NOT_DELETED` / `NOT_DELETED_E` / `NOT_DELETED_R` / `NOT_DELETED_C`; 6 repos (expenses, items, categories, accounts, subs, goals) now filter every list query through it. Validated by two `node:sqlite` dry-runs: 41 assertions across 16 trigger/backfill scenarios + 24 assertions across the 9 rewritten queries. Phase 1.C now 20/20 (complete).

- 2026-05-19 · 4.24 · Multi-image receipt stitching for long DMart / pharmacy receipts that don't fit a single photo (problems.md W3). New pure-JS module `app/src/ocr/stitchPages.js` exports `mergePages(pages)` taking an array of `parseReceipt()` outputs in capture order. Item dedup: same `normalized_name` + `|price diff| ≤ 0.01` (constant PRICE_EPS = 0.01). First-occurrence wins. Each merged item annotated with `_pageId` (source page index) + `_origIdx` (original position within that page) — required for the Scan UI's edit-preservation across re-merges. Helpers: `dedupItems(pages)`, `pickBestHeader(pages, field, defaultsToSkip)` (highest formatConfidence non-empty non-sentinel wins; skips `Unknown store` and fallback dates), `pickTotal(pages, mergedItems)` (walks pages in REVERSE — last-page total wins if > 0, else sum-of-merged-items), `pickAmount(pages, field)` (per-component tax / subtotal / cgst / sgst / igst — highest formatConfidence with non-zero value). Fees + discounts: concat (no dedup; typically unique per page). Confidence: `scoreConfidence` re-run on merged shape because per-page confidences don't compose linearly. `bands` + `columns` (4.22 template capture material): last-non-null wins. New `ScanService.scanAndProcessMore(uri, priorPages, pots)` exports the full single-page pipeline (lightPreprocess → ML Kit → optional Tesseract fallback per 4.21 → optional template apply per 4.22) on a new image, merges with priorPages, returns a review payload carrying `_pages` array, `_pageCount`, `_parsed` (the merged parsed shape), `_parsedForTemplate`. `scanAndProcess` extended with `processed._parsed = parsed` so single-page and stitched paths share one snapshot format. Scan.js gains 3 new state slots (pagesRef = useRef([]), pageCount, addingPage) + 2 new handlers (addAnotherPage, addPageFromPicker(source)). `processImage` seeds `pagesRef = [_parsed]` after first scan and tags every first-page item with `_pageId=0, _origIdx=i`. Review screen renders a new "📄 Add page N+1 · for long receipts" pill above the Retake/Save row (cream surface, disabled-while-in-flight with "Adding page…" + ActivityIndicator). On tap → Alert (Camera/Gallery/Cancel) → picker → scanAndProcessMore → pagesRef updated → setPageCount → edit-preservation merge (Map keyed by `pageId:origIdx`, user-edited rows kept in place; manually-added rows with no _pageId appended at end) → headers only overwritten when current value equals prior captureRef snapshot (skip-if-user-edited for merchant/date/total/taxInvoice). resetScreen clears pagesRef + pageCount + addingPage. UX: toast "Page N added" on success; Alert "Could not add page" on failure (never blocks save). Validated by 2 standalone Node scripts: **78/78 total** — `drift_stitch_pages_validate.mjs` 41/41 (null safety, single-page identity with _pageId tagging, 2-page merge with overlap correctness across 7 sub-assertions, last-page total wins, sum-of-items fallback when no page has total, PRICE_EPS boundary at 0.005 vs 0.02, highest-formatConfidence header pick, Unknown-store sentinel skip, GSTIN / invoice number non-null wins, per-component amounts via formatConfidence, fees/discounts concat = sum-of-pages, confidence re-scored, format mode + formatConfidence max, 3-page DMart simulation 10 unique items with grand-total wins, bands/columns last-non-null), `drift_stitch_integration_validate.mjs` 37/37 (stitchPages exports + structure + helpers, ScanService imports + scanAndProcessMore wiring + _parsed attachment, Scan.js imports + 3 state declarations + 2 handler declarations + first-page seeding + edit-preservation Map + UI button label "Add page N+1" + disabled-while-in-flight + resetScreen extensions clearing pagesRef + pageCount). **No user action needed** — pure JS + UI; no schema, no native, no deps. Phase 2.C now 4/5 (4.21, 4.22, 4.23, 4.24 done; 4.20 [-] deferred) · uncommitted · `app/src/ocr/stitchPages.js` (new, ~170 LOC), `app/src/features/scan/ScanService.js:1-10,131-138,140-180` (mergePages import + processed._parsed attach + scanAndProcessMore export), `app/src/features/scan/screens/Scan.js:8,52-58,108-115,144-243,294-296` (scanAndProcessMore import + multi-page state + processImage seeding + addAnotherPage/addPageFromPicker handlers + resetScreen extensions + "Add page" UI pill in review stage), `/tmp/drift_stitch_{pages,integration}_validate.mjs` (2 harnesses, not committed)
- 2026-05-19 · 4.23 · Pharmacy-specific item extraction. Migration v19 (`pharmacy-item-metadata`) appends 3 nullable TEXT columns to `receipt_items`: `batch_no`, `expiry_date`, `mfg_date`. Patterns: new `BATCH_RE` / `EXPIRY_RE` / `MFG_RE` in `app/src/ocr/patterns.js`, each keyword-anchored (Batch/B.No/BN/Lot · EXP/EXPIRY/Exp Dt · MFG/M.Dt/Mfd) so bare dates and random brand strings never produce false positives. Capture group 1 holds the value. Helpers in `parseReceipt.js`: `parseDrugDate(raw, mode)` parses MM/YY|MM/YYYY|DD/MM/YY|DD/MM/YYYY across `/`, `-`, `.` separators (plausibility window 1980..2099; 2-digit year window 00..79→2000s, 80..99→1900s); `normaliseExpiry`/`normaliseMfg` thin wrappers picking last-of-month vs first-of-month respectively; `extractPharmacyMeta(rows, idx, lookback=3)` scans current row + 3 rows back for batch/expiry/mfg, returning the matched values plus an array of [start,end] strip ranges for same-row hits only. New strategy `extractItemsPharmacy(rows, bands)` mirrors `extractItemsTabular` with two pharmacy-aware additions: (1) strips batch/expiry/mfg ranges from the working text BEFORE matchAmounts() so `B/24/011` no longer feeds `/011` into the amount list (root cause of the "batch numbers corrupt prices" gap from problems.md:399), (2) passes batchNo/expiryDate/mfgDate to buildItem. `buildItem` extended with three new optional fields, all defaulting to null. `FORMAT_CONFIGS.pharmacy.itemStrategy` flipped from `tabular` → `pharmacy` in detectFormat.js (restaurant/departmental unaffected). dispatchItems gains `case 'pharmacy': return extractItemsPharmacy(rows, bands)`. Persistence: both `expenses.createWithItems` and `items.replaceItems` INSERT statements extended (column list + bind values). Drug strength (e.g. "500mg"), drug form (tab/cap), schedule (H/H1/X), and Detail-screen UI surfacing are explicitly out of scope this batch — flagged in Decision log. Validated by 3 standalone Node scripts: **97/97 total** — `drift_v19_validate.mjs` 19/19 (migration ladder v1→v19, column structure + nullability + defaults, INSERT round-trip incl. NULL persistence for non-pharmacy items), `drift_pharmacy_patterns_validate.mjs` 48/48 (BATCH_RE 7 positives + 4 negatives; EXPIRY_RE 5 positives + 3 negatives; MFG_RE 4 positives + 2 negatives; normaliseExpiry 16 edge cases incl. leap-year 02/24→2024-02-29, month/year-only → last-of-month, 4-part rejection, year-out-of-band rejection; normaliseMfg 3 cases; 2-digit year window 4 edge cases at 79/80/99/00 boundaries), `drift_extractItemsPharmacy_validate.mjs` 30/30 (patterns exports, parseReceipt imports + helpers, PHARMACY_LOOKBACK=3 constant, extractPharmacyMeta strip-range tracking, dispatch case wiring, name-strip-before-matchAmounts, buildItem extension, FORMAT_CONFIGS.pharmacy flip + restaurant/departmental unaffected, both repo INSERTs) · uncommitted · `app/src/db/schema.js:643-656,762-766` (V19_SQL + migration), `app/src/ocr/patterns.js:102-128` (BATCH_RE / EXPIRY_RE / MFG_RE), `app/src/ocr/parseReceipt.js:31-33,388-409,411-503,608-685,759` (imports + buildItem extension + pharmacy helpers + extractItemsPharmacy + dispatch case), `app/src/ocr/detectFormat.js:57-67` (itemStrategy flip), `app/src/features/expenses/repo.js:165-194` (INSERT extension), `app/src/features/items/repo.js:172-201` (INSERT extension), `/tmp/drift_{v19,pharmacy_patterns,extractItemsPharmacy}_validate.mjs` (3 harnesses, not committed)
- 2026-05-19 · 4.22 · Per-merchant template learning loop. New migration v18 (`receipt-templates`) creates `receipt_templates` (id PK, merchant_id INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES merchants(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, format TEXT NOT NULL, column_map TEXT JSON, header_frac REAL DEFAULT 0.15, footer_frac REAL DEFAULT 0.20, item_start_keyword TEXT, item_end_keyword TEXT, sample_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0, outlier_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0, created_at/updated_at TEXT DEFAULTS datetime('now')); `TABLES` array gets receipt_templates inserted between expenses-side rollups and merchants (child-first FK order for resetAll). New `app/src/features/scan/templates.repo.js` exports `templates` object: `getByMerchant(merchantId) → row|null`, `list()`, `removeByMerchant(merchantId)`, `ACTIVATION_SAMPLES = 3`, `recordSample({merchantId, parsed})` (insert on first sample with parsed bands/format/columns; subsequent samples weighted-mean header/footer_frac using `(mean*n + value*w)/(n+w)`, with 40% relative-deviation triggering outlier weight 0.25 + bumping outlier_count; format mode locks to existing after n>=2 so a late detectFormat misclassification can't flip a stable learned format; column_map carries forward from latest non-outlier sample) + `parseColumnMap(json)` helper returns null on any parse failure so callers can silently fall back to per-receipt detectColumns. `parseReceipt(ocrResultOrLines, options = {})` accepts second arg; when `options.template` present: format swapped via `FORMAT_CONFIGS[template.format]` (formatConfidence floor 0.8), bands swapped via `itemBandTop = minY + rangeY*header_frac`, `itemBandBottom = minY + rangeY*(1-footer_frac)` (gated at 0 <= frac < 0.5), columnsOverride threaded through dispatchItems → extractItemsColumnar (used when override has ≥ COLUMNAR_MIN_COLS columns, skipping per-receipt detectColumns). Parser also exposes `parsed.bands = {header_frac, footer_frac}` (rounded to 3 decimals) and `parsed.columns = [{x0,x1}]` so the capture path knows what to store. `ScanService.scanAndProcess` rewired with the apply path between probe-parse and the 4.21 Tesseract fallback: resolves merchant via `merchants.resolve(parsed.merchant)` (one extra round-trip — may INSERT a new merchants row if first scan of merchant, idempotent with save-time resolve), looks up template, and when `sample_count >= templates.ACTIVATION_SAMPLES` re-parses with template; only swaps when templated `confidence.overall >= probe` (same regression-safety as 4.21). The 4.21 fallback path now also passes the template option so both branches benefit. Attaches `processed.{merchantId, templateApplied, _parsedForTemplate}` to the review payload — last is internal-only (underscore-prefixed) for the capture call. `expenses.createWithItems` extended with a one-line bypass: `expense.merchant_id != null ? expense.merchant_id : await merchants.resolve(expense.merchant)` so a scan-time-resolved id flows through the transaction without a second JW match. `Scan.js` gains a `templateRef = useRef({merchantId, parsedForTemplate})` parallel to the 4.19 captureRef, plumbs `merchant_id` into `buildSavePayload`'s expense object, and fires `receiptTemplates.recordSample({merchantId, parsed: parsedForTemplate}).catch(() => {})` after a successful save — same fire-and-forget discipline as the 4.19 golden capture. Validated by 3 standalone Node scripts: **103/103 total** — `drift_v18_validate.mjs` 27/27 (migration ladder v1→v18, table structure, UNIQUE constraint on merchant_id, FK ON DELETE CASCADE behavior, default values, insert + select round-trip, updated_at tick), `drift_templates_validate.mjs` 43/43 (full upsert sequence: insert → 2nd-3rd running mean → activation gate at 3 → outlier weighting at 40% relative deviation with weight 0.25 (manually checked `(0.12*3 + 0.50*0.25)/3.25 ≈ 0.1492`) → format mode lock-in → multi-merchant independence → null safety → bands-missing fallback → list/remove), `drift_parseReceipt_template_validate.mjs` 33/33 (structural contract: parseReceipt(ocrResultOrLines, options={}) signature, FORMAT_CONFIGS import, template.format override, sane-value gates, columnsOverride threading through dispatchItems → extractItemsColumnar(rows, bands, opts={}), parsed.bands + parsed.columns exposure, ScanService apply/capture wiring, Scan.js merchant_id passthrough, expenses.createWithItems bypass). Native-side changes: none — pure SQL + JS. **No user action needed** beyond the next app launch which auto-applies v18 · uncommitted · `app/src/db/schema.js:657-695,769-784` (V18_SQL + migration + TABLES insert), `app/src/features/scan/templates.repo.js` (new), `app/src/ocr/parseReceipt.js:6,648-666,754,808-873,895-918` (FORMAT_CONFIGS import + extractItemsColumnar opts + dispatchItems extra + orchestrator options arg + template overrides + bands/columns exposure), `app/src/features/scan/ScanService.js:1-8,99-176` (imports + apply/capture wiring + safeGetTemplate), `app/src/features/expenses/repo.js:128-142` (merchant_id bypass), `app/src/features/scan/screens/Scan.js:14,46-51,113-118,182-189,217-228` (import + templateRef + capture + payload merchant_id + recordSample fire-and-forget), `/tmp/drift_{v18,templates,parseReceipt_template}_validate.mjs` (3 harnesses, not committed)
- 2026-05-19 · 4.21 · Tesseract LSTM fallback OCR — three-tier stack closed at Tier 2. New JS: `app/src/ocr/fallback.js` (`shouldFallback(parsed, lines)` triggers on lines<5 OR overall<0.5 OR (items=0 AND total<=0) OR (Unknown-merchant AND <15 lines); `mergeEngineResults(mlkit, tess)` does y-overlap pairing with ≥0.5 ratio, swaps in Tesseract text when it carries non-Latin glyphs and ML Kit version is pure-Latin (Devanagari/Tamil/Telugu/Bengali blocks tested), keeps ML Kit frame even on swap, appends Tesseract-only orphans, sorts top-to-bottom-left-to-right). `app/src/ocr/tesseract.js` — `TesseractEngine.{available,init,recognize}` wraps `NativeModules.Tesseract` with `{available:false, lines:[]}` sentinel on Expo Go / web / iOS / pre-rebuild so the fallback gracefully skips when the native module isn't linked. `parseReceipt(ocrResultOrLines)` accepts either an ML Kit result or a pre-extracted line array (one-line `Array.isArray` short-circuit at the orchestrator top — required by the merge path). `processReceipt` split into pure `buildReviewPayload(parsed, pots)` + back-compat wrapper. `ScanService.scanAndProcess` orchestrates: lightPreprocess → mlKit → extractLines → probe parseReceipt → if shouldFallback: TesseractEngine.recognize → mergeEngineResults → reparseReceipt → only swap when merged confidence ≥ prior (regression-safety); attaches `parsed.engine = 'mlkit' | 'mlkit+tesseract'` and propagates to `processed.engine`. `golden/capture.js` extended: `processed.engine === 'mlkit+tesseract'` adds +1 to capture-score and writes the `engine` field into the candidate payload so the 4.19 corpus can quantify fallback efficacy once it grows. Native: `app/android/app/src/main/java/com/drift/expensemanager/TesseractModule.kt` (140 LOC, single-threaded executor, lazy assets→filesDir copy on first init, `RIL_TEXTLINE` iterator returns text+bbox+confidence/100) + `TesseractPackage.kt` + `MainApplication.kt` registers `TesseractPackage()`. Gradle: `com.github.adaptech-cz.Tesseract4Android:4.7.0` via jitpack (root repo already configured). ProGuard: `com.googlecode.{tesseract,leptonica}.android.**` keep + dontwarn. Language packs (`eng.traineddata`, `hin.traineddata` from tessdata_best) fetched via new `app/scripts/download_tessdata.sh` — gitignored (whole `/android` tree). Validated by `drift_fallback_validate.mjs`: **28/28 pure-JS assertions** (9 shouldFallback paths + 19 mergeEngineResults cases incl. Devanagari swap, Latin-Latin keep, orphan-append, partial-overlap below threshold, null safety). Native module untestable in Node — requires user device verification · uncommitted · `app/src/ocr/{fallback,tesseract}.js` (new), `app/src/ocr/parseReceipt.js:793-803` (accept-lines dispatch), `app/src/features/scan/ScanService.js:1-7, 38-72, 86-136`, `app/src/ocr/golden/capture.js:67-75, 175`, `app/android/app/src/main/java/com/drift/expensemanager/TesseractModule.kt` (new), `…/TesseractPackage.kt` (new), `…/MainApplication.kt:25-29`, `app/android/app/build.gradle:198-203`, `app/android/app/proguard-rules.pro:77-84`, `app/scripts/download_tessdata.sh` (new), `app/android/app/src/main/assets/tessdata/` (new dir, traineddata files gitignored), `/tmp/drift_fallback_validate.mjs` (harness, not committed)
- 2026-05-19 · 5.9 + 5.10 · MerchantDetail + autocomplete + alias learning loop. Migration v24 (`merchant-aliases`) appended — table with `alias_key TEXT NOT NULL`, nullable `merchant_id`/`category_id` FKs (both SET NULL), `source` CHECK ('user'|'bundle'), `created_at`/`updated_at` DEFAULTS, `deleted_at`, plus a partial `UNIQUE INDEX (alias_key, source) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL` so soft-deleted history doesn't collide with a fresh user choice and a `merchant_id` partial index. `TABLES` array gets `merchant_aliases` inserted between `receipt_templates` and `merchants` so the children-first contract holds (both FKs are SET NULL so the order is correctness-by-convention, not enforcement). New `app/src/features/expenses/merchantMap.json` ships 58 popular Indian merchants pre-canonicalised through `lightNormMerchant()` and partitioned across 7 STARTER_CATEGORIES (Food & Drink / Groceries / Transport / Health / Subscriptions / Bills / Fun). New `app/src/features/expenses/aliases.repo.js` (~130 LOC) — two-tier lookup: SELECT user-source WHERE alias_key=? AND deleted_at IS NULL first; on miss, longest-key-first substring search of the bundle JSON, then case-insensitive name match against `categories` to derive a usable category_id (returns null if no live category matches). `recordUserChoice` does SELECT-then-UPDATE/INSERT against the partial-unique index, refreshing `updated_at` so the most-recent choice wins. `clearUserAliases` reserved for a future "forget merchants" affordance. `merchants.repo.js` extends with `suggest(prefix, {limit})` — SQL pass (exact + prefix + contains with rank CASE-WHEN ordered by length asc) cap-3× the limit then JW≥0.78 fallback only when SQL underflows + `resolveExisting(name)` that does the resolve()-style match without ever inserting (safer for the autocomplete path). `expenses.repo.js` extends with 5 merchant queries + `lastAtMerchant`: `topMerchants` (SUM/COUNT/MAX, joined-merchants-only, indexed `month_key` window, ordered total DESC ties → count DESC → last_seen DESC), `merchantSummary` (single-row CASE-WHEN aggregation: total_all/total_window/txn_count_all/avg/first_seen/last_seen), `merchantMonthlyTrend` (12 months via month_key — joined directly against expenses since monthly_summary is category-bucketed, not merchant-bucketed), `merchantCategoryBreakdown` (LEFT JOIN preserves NULL-category bucket as `id=null`), `merchantRecents` (Detail-row shape with category join, soft-delete-aware, LIMIT clamp). `expenses.create()` re-resolves `merchant_id` so manual Add path stops leaving the FK NULL; `expenses.update()` re-resolves only when the merchant text changes (avoids spurious resolution on amount-only edits). New `app/src/features/expenses/screens/MerchantDetail.js` mirrors `ItemTrend`: hero card with name + window total + visits + avg + last_seen, 12-month tappable bar chart with selected-bar callout in F.cream, per-category breakdown with horizontal share bar + percentage label, recent spends list tapping into Detail. New `app/src/features/expenses/screens/Merchants.js` leaderboard with 4-range pill (3mo/6mo/12mo/All=600mo proxy), pull-to-refresh, ranked 1..50 list, empty state for cold-start. Both wired into `navigation/index.js` via `withBoundary` HOC. `Detail.js`'s merchant text becomes tappable (dotted underline) only when `e.merchant_id != null` — legacy NULL-id rows stay non-interactive. `Profile.js` gains "🏪 Top merchants" row in the Items & Trends section pointing at `Merchants`. `Add.js`: new `merchantSuggestions/showSuggestions` state + `pickedMerchantIdRef` + `userTouchedCategoryRef` + `suggestReqIdRef` (stale-request guard) + `debounceRef` (180ms). `onMerchantChange` debounces `merchantRepo.suggest`; the dropdown lists up to 5 results below the TextInput in both Quick (hero card) and Detailed (merchant field) layouts; `onMerchantBlur` and `pickSuggestion` both fire `aliases.lookup` and silently swap `potId` ONLY when the user hasn't tapped a category pill (the pill tap permanently locks the manual choice for the session). Save threads the picked merchant_id through and fires `aliases.recordUserChoice` fire-and-forget after success so the user's latest decision overrides the bundle next time. `ExpensesProvider.addExpense/addExpenseWithItems` now return the saved row (additive — every caller was previously discarding the return value). Migration **v27 `analytics-cache`** is pure `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS analytics_cache (key TEXT PK, scope TEXT NOT NULL, value TEXT NOT NULL, expires_at TEXT NOT NULL, created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT datetime('now'))` + `idx_ac_scope_expires(scope, expires_at)`. New `app/src/analytics/cache.js` exports `getCached(key, ttlSec, computeFn, {scope})` (read-through, INSERT OR REPLACE on miss, JSON-encoded value, TTL rendered as absolute `expires_at` for O(1) reads), `invalidate(scopes[])`, `invalidateKey(key)`, `evictExpired()`, `stats()`, plus a frozen `SCOPES` enum (8 values). 6.2 scaffolds `app/src/analytics/{index,spend,items,subscriptions,lifestyle,forecast,seasonal,anomaly,patterns}.js` — the four unimplemented modules export a single `notImplemented()` throw and are NOT re-exported from `index.js`. **6.3** `spendingVelocity()` (spend.js): one SQL pass, CASE-bucketed 14-day window, ±15% PoP classifier (accelerating/slowing/steady) with explicit `insufficient` when both windows zero. **6.8** `subscriptionLeakage()` (subscriptions.js): live subs only (`cancelled=0 AND deleted_at IS NULL`), `periodToMonthly()` regex map (mo/wk/yr/q/d + unknown→monthly), denominator from `monthly_summary` current-month rollup, returns `{month_key, monthly_subs_total, monthly_spend_total, leakage_ratio, subs_count, period_breakdown, top_subs}` — `period_breakdown` surfaces silently-assumed-monthly subs for the 6.12 UI. **6.6** `reorderQueue()` (items.js): `item_summary.points_count >= 3` gating, all live purchase_dates pulled in one query (`idx_items_name_date`), JS-side avg interval + `due_in_days = last_seen + avg − today`, statuses overdue/imminent/upcoming. **6.5** `cheapestMerchantPerItem()` (items.js): GROUP BY (normalized_name, merchant) with `HAVING samples >= 2`, JOIN expenses for merchant string, per-item top-3. **6.4** `inflationBasket()` (items.js): per-item per-month avg price → top-20 by frequency → weight `min(0.10, freq_share)` then renormalise → base = first month with ≥5 basket items → per-month index `Σ(w · p/p_base) / Σ(w_used)` over items present in both, base index = 1.00; returns `{ ready: false, reason }` shapes when data thin (no_priced_items/no_basket/no_base_month/base_month_thin). **6.7** `lifestyleInflation()` (lifestyle.js): SQLite `date('now','-N month')` resolves quarter ranges (this_q = [-2mo, now], prev_q = [-5mo, -3mo]), sums `monthly_summary` per category × quarter, computes `share_drift = this_share − prev_share`, flags `|drift| ≥ 5pp AND prev_share ≥ 5%`. Returns both share-drift + absolute deltas. Validation: **`/tmp/drift_v27_validate.mjs`** extracts `V27_SQL` from schema.js via regex, applies to in-memory node:sqlite, asserts table + index + 5 columns exist, INSERT/SELECT round-trip with expires_at-future, eviction of expires_at-past row, INSERT-OR-REPLACE overwrite — **PASS**. **`/tmp/drift_analytics_validate.mjs`** seeds 3 categories × 6 months expenses, 7 items × multi-month prices, 4 subs (3 active + 1 cancelled across mo/yr/wk periods), exercises all 6 functions with inlined logic against node:sqlite, asserts specific outputs: 6.3 current=5000/prior=3000/accelerating, 6.8 ₹1903.5 monthly (500+100+1303.5) / ₹15000 = 12.69%, cancelled-Old excluded; 6.5 milk→store_B beats store_A, single-sample store_C excluded; 6.6 5 items in queue, 2-buy rice excluded, milk due_in_days=+7; 6.4 base_month=2026-03 (Feb has 3 items, March is first with ≥5), final index=1.147 reflects 10-15% drift; 6.7 food +15.3pp flagged, bills -16.1pp flagged, shop +0.9pp not flagged. () => setMoodOn(false) : undefined}` (Clear only when engaged so the chip-state doesn't show a vestigial link). Both quick-mode and detailed-mode save paths write `mood: moodOn ? MOODS[moodIdx] : null` — fresh expenses with no mood touch now store NULL instead of the legacy silent 😌. `EditExpense.js` applies the same dual-state with `moodOn = !!e?.mood` seeded from the existing row, so moded rows open expanded with their stored face selected (Clear visible) and unmoded rows open as the chip. MOODS.indexOf(e.mood) : 1` fallback line is intentionally untouched (Step 2 Q4) — it still produces a sensible index when the picker is expanded, and `moodOn` independently gates whether that index reaches the DB. `Scan.js` L318 hard-coded `mood: '😌'` flipped to `mood: null` so OCR-saved expenses no longer get a silent stamp; no UI added to Scan review (would be a new affordance, deferred). **No schema migration** — `expenses.mood TEXT` was already nullable at v1 and `expRepo.create` already coerces `undefined → null` (`mood ?? null` at repo.js L81). - 2026-05-19 · 5.13 + 5.14 + 5.15 · Receipt viewer + predictive chip + reader flip + lazy migrate shipped in one batch. 5.13: new `app/src/features/expenses/components/ReceiptViewer.js` — full-screen `Modal` (transparent, rgba(0,0,0,0.92) backdrop, animationType='fade') hosting a `GestureDetector` over `Animated.View` from RNGH 2.28 + Reanimated 4.1 (both already installed). Gestures composed via `Gesture.Simultaneous(pinch, Gesture.Race(doubleTap, pan))`: pinch bounded `[1, 4]` with snap-back to 1× when below 1.05; pan only active when scale > 1.05, deltas clamped against `(scale-1) * dim / 2` so the image can't leave the canvas; double-tap toggles 1× ↔ 2×. All RNGH + Reanimated imports lazy-required so a dev shell missing the native rebuild falls through to a static contain-fit Image. Detail.js renders a new "Receipt" card between the Tax-invoice card and the Items list when `e.receipt_path || e.receipt_uri` is set — 64×88 thumb (cover fit, F.cream placeholder when no thumb) + "Tap to view · Pinch to zoom · drag to pan" hint + chevron; tap opens the viewer Modal. 5.15: new shared pure-JS module `app/src/features/expenses/receiptUri.js` exports `pickReceiptUri(e) → {full, thumb}` (prefers permanent path/thumb, falls back to legacy uri), `hasReceipt(e)`, `needsMigration(e)`. New repo method `expenses.attachReceiptStorage(id, {path, thumb, bytes})` UPDATEs the v7 columns in-place — leaves `receipt_uri` intact (safety net if future expo upgrades shift documentDirectory). Lazy-migrate hook lives in ReceiptViewer.js's open-side effect: when `needsMigration(row)` returns true, fires `persistReceipt(row.receipt_uri)` → `expRepo.attachReceiptStorage(...)`, surfaces a "Saving permanently…" inline pill while running, calls `onMigrated` to bubble the new URIs into Detail's local row-shadow state so the thumb refreshes without a provider hop. `filters.js` `hasReceipt` predicate flipped from `e.receipt_uri IS [NOT] NULL` to `COALESCE(e.receipt_path, e.receipt_uri) IS [NOT] NULL` so saved-filter rows match both legacy + migrated rows uniformly. 5.14: Add.js gains `lastSpend` + `chipApplied` state + `lookupLastSpend(merchantId)` that fires `expRepo.lastAtMerchant(merchantId)` (already added by 5.9). Two trigger points: `pickSuggestion(s)` (autocomplete tap, knows the id directly) and `applyAliasIfAny(name, merchantId)` (free-typed merchant that resolved through bundle/user alias). Inline `LastSpendChip` sub-component renders below the merchant input in BOTH Quick + Detailed layouts: F.cream pill `🕒 Last time: ₹420 · 12 May`. Tap calls `formatAmountForKeypad(lastSpend.amount).toFixed(2)` and sets `chipApplied=true` (chip flips to coral + ✓); second tap clears amount back to `'0.00'` and resets `chipApplied`. Manual keypad press cancels `chipApplied` (so a subsequent tap on the chip re-fills). Visibility predicate: `!isIncome && !!lastSpend && (parseFloat(amount) === 0 || chipApplied)` — so typing past the chip hides it until cleared, but the applied-then-edit state keeps it visible while the user adjusts. Merchant text changing through onMerchantChange clears `lastSpend` + `chipApplied` so a new merchant rehydrates from scratch. New helpers `formatAmountForKeypad(n)` (clamps to '0.00' on NaN/negative/Infinity/string; `toFixed(2)` otherwise — matches the keypad's two-decimal pad-and-shift state) and `formatChipDate(iso)` (YYYY-MM-DD → "12 May") exported for testability. Validated by 3 standalone Node scripts: **63/63 total** — `/tmp/drift_5_13_pickreceipt_pure.mjs` 21/21 (all 8 (path/thumb/uri) combinations + null safety + hasReceipt incl. thumb-only=false + needsMigration including empty-uri=false), `/tmp/drift_5_14_chip_pure.mjs` 28/28 (formatAmountForKeypad across 11 inputs incl. Infinity/NaN/null/negative/string; formatChipDate across 8 incl. malformed; tap-state machine 4-step trace incl. keypad-cancel; visibility predicate 5 cases incl. income mode + applied-then-edit), `/tmp/drift_5_15_migrate_validate.mjs` 14/14 via `node:sqlite` (4-row corpus: legacy/migrated/no-receipt/soft-deleted-legacy; pre-migrate hasReceipt=true matches legacy+migrated, hasReceipt=false matches no-receipt, soft-delete excluded; attachReceiptStorage stamps path/thumb/bytes while retaining receipt_uri; idempotent re-attach overwrites; null-path no-op; null-thumb permitted; null/missing id no-op). Babel parse-check across all 6 touched files passes via `@babel/parser`. **No user action needed** — pure JS + UI, no schema change (v7 already covered the columns), no new dependencies (RNGH + Reanimated already installed), no native rebuild.
